<file path="ViewModels/Base/ViewModelBase.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System.Windows;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;

public abstract partial class ViewModelBase : ObservableObject
{
    [ObservableProperty]
    private bool _isBusy;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _statusMessage = string.Empty;

    protected static void ShowError(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "خطأ", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);

    protected static void ShowSuccess(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "تم", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);

    protected static bool Confirm(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "تأكيد", MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Question) == MessageBoxResult.Yes;
}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/EditInvoiceViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

/// <summary>
/// ViewModel لتعديل فاتورة موجودة.
/// يفتح كـ Dialog من InvoiceListViewModel أو SafeReportViewModel.
/// ما يسمح بتعديله: التاريخ، الخصم، الأصناف اليدوية (اسم/سعر/كمية)، الملاحظات.
/// الأصناف المرتبطة بكيس دم لا تُعدَّل (تحتاج إلغاء كامل للفاتورة).
/// </summary>
public partial class EditInvoiceViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Header
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [ObservableProperty] private int _invoiceDbId;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _invoiceNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _customerName = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _date = DateTime.Today;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _notes = string.Empty;

    // ─── نوع الدفع (للعرض فقط — لا يتغير) ────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private string _paymentTypeDisplay = string.Empty;

    // ─── الخصم ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(TotalAmount))]
    private decimal _discountAmount;

    // ─── الإجمالي (محسوب) ────────────────────────────────────
    public decimal SubTotal => Lines.Sum(l => l.Total);
    public decimal TotalAmount => Math.Max(0, SubTotal - DiscountAmount);

    // ─── القيم الأصلية قبل التعديل (لحساب الفرق) ─────────────
    private decimal _originalDiscountAmount;
    private decimal _originalTotalAmount;
    private decimal _originalPaidAmount;
    private DateTime _originalDate;
    private string _originalNotes = string.Empty;

    // ─── صناديق PIN ───────────────────────────────────────────
    private string _ownerPinHash = string.Empty;

    // ─── بيانات الخزينة / العميل (للتسوية المالية) ────────────
    private int? _safeId;
    private int _customerId;
    private PaymentType _paymentType;
    private decimal _originalPaidAmountForJournal;

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Lines
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    public ObservableCollection<EditableInvoiceLine> Lines { get; } = [];

    // ── إضافة صنف يدوي جديد ──────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private string _newLineName = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private int _newLineQty = 1;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private decimal _newLineUnitPrice;

    public decimal NewLineTotal => NewLineQty * NewLineUnitPrice;

    public IReadOnlyList<string> BloodProductTypes { get; } =
    [
        "دم كامل",
        "كرات دم مكدسه",
        "بلازما طازجه مجمده",
        "كرايو - راسب اببض",
        "٦ وحدات صفائح دموية",
        " وحدة صفائح",
        "فلترة كيس دم",
        "غسيل كيس دم",
        "كيس دم اطفال",
        "كرات دم مكدسه موجب",
        "كرات دم مكدسه سالب"
    ];

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Result
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    /// <summary>يُضبط على true بعد الحفظ الناجح</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _saved;

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Load
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    public EditInvoiceViewModel(int invoiceId)
    {
        InvoiceDbId = invoiceId;
        _ = LoadAsync(invoiceId);
    }

    private async Task LoadAsync(int invoiceId)
    {
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();

            var inv = await db.Invoices
                .Include(i => i.Customer)
                .Include(i => i.Lines)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(i => i.Id == invoiceId);

            if (inv is null) { ShowError("لم يتم العثور على الفاتورة."); return; }
            if (inv.IsCancelled) { ShowError("لا يمكن تعديل فاتورة ملغاة."); return; }

            var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
            _ownerPinHash = org?.OwnerPinHash ?? string.Empty;

            // ── حفظ القيم الأصلية ────────────────────────────
            _originalDate = inv.Date;
            _originalDiscountAmount = inv.DiscountAmount;
            _originalTotalAmount = inv.TotalAmount;
            _originalPaidAmount = inv.PaidAmount;
            _originalNotes = inv.Notes ?? string.Empty;
            _safeId = inv.SafeId;
            _customerId = inv.CustomerId;
            _paymentType = inv.PaymentType;
            _originalPaidAmountForJournal = inv.PaidAmount;

            // ── ملء الـ UI ────────────────────────────────────
            InvoiceNumber = inv.InvoiceNumber;
            CustomerName = inv.Customer.Name;
            Date = inv.Date;
            DiscountAmount = inv.DiscountAmount;
            Notes = inv.Notes ?? string.Empty;
            PaymentTypeDisplay = inv.PaymentType switch
            {
                PaymentType.Cash => "نقدي",
                PaymentType.Credit => "آجل",
                PaymentType.Partial => "جزئي",
                PaymentType.Electronic => "إلكتروني",
                _ => inv.PaymentType.ToString()
            };

            Lines.Clear();
            foreach (var line in inv.Lines)
            {
                var el = new EditableInvoiceLine
                {
                    Id = line.Id,
                    BloodBagId = line.BloodBagId,
                    IsManual = !line.BloodBagId.HasValue,
                    ProductName = line.ProductName,
                    UnitPrice = line.UnitPrice,
                    Quantity = line.Quantity,
                    IsOriginal = true
                };
                el.PropertyChanged += (_, _) =>
                {
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SubTotal));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalAmount));
                };
                Lines.Add(el);
            }

            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SubTotal));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalAmount));
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Lines Commands
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [RelayCommand]
    private void AddManualLine()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NewLineName))
        { ShowError("أدخل اسم الصنف"); return; }
        if (NewLineQty <= 0)
        { ShowError("الكمية يجب أن تكون أكبر من صفر"); return; }
        if (NewLineUnitPrice <= 0)
        { ShowError("أدخل سعر الوحدة"); return; }

        var el = new EditableInvoiceLine
        {
            Id = 0,   // جديد — لم يُحفظ بعد
            BloodBagId = null,
            IsManual = true,
            IsOriginal = false,
            ProductName = NewLineName.Trim(),
            UnitPrice = NewLineUnitPrice,
            Quantity = NewLineQty
        };
        el.PropertyChanged += (_, _) =>
        {
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SubTotal));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalAmount));
        };
        Lines.Add(el);

        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SubTotal));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalAmount));

        NewLineName = string.Empty;
        NewLineQty = 1;
        NewLineUnitPrice = 0;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void RemoveLine(EditableInvoiceLine line)
    {
        // لا تحذف أصناف مرتبطة بكيس دم — تحتاج إلغاء كامل
        if (line.BloodBagId.HasValue)
        {
            ShowError("لا يمكن حذف صنف مرتبط بكيس دم.\nلإزالته يجب إلغاء الفاتورة كاملاً وإعادة الإصدار.");
            return;
        }
        Lines.Remove(line);
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SubTotal));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalAmount));
    }

    partial void OnDiscountAmountChanged(decimal value)
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalAmount));
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Validation
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private bool ValidateBeforeSave()
    {
        if (!Lines.Any())
        { ShowError("يجب أن تحتوي الفاتورة على صنف واحد على الأقل."); return false; }

        if (DiscountAmount < 0)
        { ShowError("الخصم لا يمكن أن يكون قيمة سالبة."); return false; }

        if (DiscountAmount >= SubTotal && SubTotal > 0)
        { ShowError("الخصم لا يمكن أن يساوي أو يتجاوز إجمالي الأصناف."); return false; }

        foreach (var line in Lines)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line.ProductName))
            { ShowError("اسم الصنف لا يمكن أن يكون فارغاً."); return false; }
            if (line.Quantity <= 0)
            { ShowError($"كمية الصنف «{line.ProductName}» يجب أن تكون أكبر من صفر."); return false; }
            if (line.UnitPrice < 0)
            { ShowError($"سعر الصنف «{line.ProductName}» لا يمكن أن يكون سالباً."); return false; }
        }

        if (Date > DateTime.Today)
        { ShowError("تاريخ الفاتورة لا يمكن أن يكون في المستقبل."); return false; }

        return true;
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Save
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        // ── 1. Validation ─────────────────────────────────────
        if (!ValidateBeforeSave()) return;

        // ── 2. التحقق من وجود أي تغيير فعلي ──────────────────
        bool hasChange = Date != _originalDate
                      || DiscountAmount != _originalDiscountAmount
                      || Notes.Trim() != _originalNotes.Trim()
                      || HasLineChanges();

        if (!hasChange)
        {
            ShowError("لم تقم بأي تعديل على الفاتورة.");
            return;
        }

        // ── 3. طلب الرمز السري ────────────────────────────────
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash))
        {
            var enteredPin = Views.PinDialog.Show(
                System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow);
            if (enteredPin is null) return;   // ألغى

            if (!PinHelper.Verify(enteredPin, _ownerPinHash))
            {
                ShowError("الرمز السري غير صحيح. لم يتم التعديل.");
                return;
            }
        }

        // ── 4. ملخص التغييرات للتأكيد الأول ──────────────────
        var summaryLines = BuildChangeSummary();

        if (!Confirm(
            $"مراجعة التعديلات على الفاتورة {InvoiceNumber}\n" +
            $"العميل: {CustomerName}\n\n" +
            summaryLines +
            "\n\nهل تريد المتابعة؟"))
            return;

        // ── 5. تأكيد ثانٍ نهائي ───────────────────────────────
        var newTotal = TotalAmount;
        var diff = newTotal - _originalTotalAmount;
        var diffText = diff == 0 ? "لا تغيير في الإجمالي"
                     : diff > 0 ? $"زيادة الإجمالي بمقدار {diff:N2} جنيه"
                     : $"تخفيض الإجمالي بمقدار {Math.Abs(diff):N2} جنيه";

        if (!Confirm(
            $"⚠️ تأكيد نهائي — هذا الإجراء لا يمكن التراجع عنه\n\n" +
            $"الفاتورة: {InvoiceNumber}\n" +
            $"الإجمالي القديم: {_originalTotalAmount:N2} جنيه\n" +
            $"الإجمالي الجديد: {newTotal:N2} جنيه\n" +
            $"الأثر المالي: {diffText}\n\n" +
            "هل أنت متأكد تماماً من تنفيذ هذا التعديل؟"))
            return;

        // ── 6. الحفظ في قاعدة البيانات ────────────────────────
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            var inv = await db.Invoices
                .Include(i => i.Lines)
                .Include(i => i.JournalEntries)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(i => i.Id == InvoiceDbId);

            if (inv is null) { ShowError("لم يتم العثور على الفاتورة."); return; }
            if (inv.IsCancelled) { ShowError("لا يمكن تعديل فاتورة ملغاة."); return; }

            // ── a. تسوية الخزينة / العميل ──────────────────────
            await ReconcileFinancialsAsync(db, inv, newTotal);

            // ── b. حذف القيود القديمة وإعادة تسجيلها ─────────
            if (inv.JournalEntries.Any())
                db.JournalEntries.RemoveRange(inv.JournalEntries);

            // ── c. تحديث الفاتورة ──────────────────────────────
            inv.Date = Date;
            inv.DiscountAmount = DiscountAmount;
            inv.SubTotal = SubTotal;
            inv.TotalAmount = newTotal;
            inv.Notes = Notes.Trim();
            // PaidAmount لا يتغير تلقائياً إلا في حالة Cash حيث Paid = Total
            if (inv.PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash)
                inv.PaidAmount = newTotal;

            // ── d. تحديث الأصناف ───────────────────────────────
            await SyncLinesAsync(db, inv);

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── e. إعادة تسجيل القيود المحاسبية ──────────────
            var customer = await db.Customers.FindAsync(_customerId);
            inv.Customer = customer!;

            // Load a fallback Safe (main safe) for cases where SafeId may be null
            Safe? fallbackSafe = await db.Safes.FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.IsMain);

            if (inv.PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial)
            {
                var partialSafe = inv.SafeId.HasValue
                    ? await db.Safes.FindAsync(inv.SafeId.Value)
                    : fallbackSafe;
                if (partialSafe is not null)
                    JournalService.PostPartialInvoice(db, inv, inv.PaidAmount, partialSafe);
            }
            else if (inv.PaymentType == PaymentType.Electronic && inv.SafeId.HasValue)
            {
                var elecSafe = await db.Safes.FindAsync(inv.SafeId.Value);
                if (elecSafe is not null)
                    JournalService.PostInvoice(db, inv, elecSafe);
            }
            else
            {
                // Cash → uses SafeId's safe (or main safe); Credit → PostInvoice uses AccountsRec branch, safe is ignored
                var cashSafe = inv.SafeId.HasValue
                    ? await db.Safes.FindAsync(inv.SafeId.Value)
                    : fallbackSafe;
                if (cashSafe is not null)
                    JournalService.PostInvoice(db, inv, cashSafe);
            }

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── f. حفظ سجل التدقيق ────────────────────────────
            var financialImpact = diff == 0
                ? "لا تغيير في الإجمالي"
                : diff > 0
                    ? $"زيادة {diff:N2} جنيه"
                    : $"تخفيض {Math.Abs(diff):N2} جنيه";

            db.InvoiceAuditLogs.Add(new InvoiceAuditLog
            {
                InvoiceId = InvoiceDbId,
                EditedAt = DateTime.Now,
                OldDate = _originalDate,
                OldDiscountAmount = _originalDiscountAmount,
                OldTotalAmount = _originalTotalAmount,
                OldPaidAmount = _originalPaidAmount,
                OldNotes = _originalNotes,
                NewDate = Date,
                NewDiscountAmount = DiscountAmount,
                NewTotalAmount = newTotal,
                NewPaidAmount = inv.PaidAmount,
                NewNotes = Notes.Trim(),
                FinancialImpactSummary = financialImpact
            });

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            await tx.CommitAsync();

            Saved = true;
            ShowSuccess($"تم تعديل الفاتورة {InvoiceNumber} بنجاح ✓\n{financialImpact}");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            var msg = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message;
            ShowError($"حدث خطأ أثناء التعديل:\n{msg}");
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Helpers
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    /// <summary>
    /// تسوية الخزينة ورصيد العميل بناءً على الفرق بين الإجمالي القديم والجديد.
    /// </summary>
    private async Task ReconcileFinancialsAsync(AppDbContext db, Invoice inv, decimal newTotal)
    {
        var diff = newTotal - _originalTotalAmount;
        if (diff == 0) return;

        switch (inv.PaymentType)
        {
            case PaymentType.Cash:
            case PaymentType.Electronic:
                // نقدي أو إلكتروني — نضبط الخزينة بالفرق
                if (inv.SafeId.HasValue)
                {
                    var safe = await db.Safes.FindAsync(inv.SafeId.Value);
                    if (safe is not null)
                        safe.Balance += diff;
                }
                break;

            case PaymentType.Credit:
                var custCredit = await db.Customers.FindAsync(inv.CustomerId);
                if (custCredit is not null)
                    custCredit.Balance += diff;
                break;

            case PaymentType.Partial:
                var custPartial = await db.Customers.FindAsync(inv.CustomerId);
                if (custPartial is not null)
                    custPartial.Balance += diff;
                break;

        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// مزامنة الأصناف: حذف المحذوفة، تحديث الموجودة، إضافة الجديدة.
    /// الأصناف المرتبطة بكيس دم تُحدَّث فقط (لا تُحذف).
    /// </summary>
    private Task SyncLinesAsync(AppDbContext db, Invoice inv)
    {
        var uiIds = Lines
            .Where(l => l.Id > 0)
            .Select(l => l.Id)
            .ToHashSet();

        // حذف الأصناف التي أُزيلت من الـ UI (يدوية فقط)
        foreach (var dbLine in inv.Lines.ToList())
        {
            if (!uiIds.Contains(dbLine.Id) && !dbLine.BloodBagId.HasValue)
                db.InvoiceLines.Remove(dbLine);
        }

        foreach (var uiLine in Lines)
        {
            if (uiLine.Id > 0)
            {
                // تحديث صنف موجود
                var dbLine = inv.Lines.FirstOrDefault(l => l.Id == uiLine.Id);
                if (dbLine is not null)
                {
                    dbLine.ProductName = uiLine.ProductName;
                    dbLine.UnitPrice = uiLine.UnitPrice;
                    dbLine.Quantity = uiLine.Quantity;
                    dbLine.Total = uiLine.Total;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                // إضافة صنف جديد
                db.InvoiceLines.Add(new InvoiceLine
                {
                    InvoiceId = inv.Id,
                    BloodBagId = null,
                    ProductName = uiLine.ProductName,
                    UnitPrice = uiLine.UnitPrice,
                    Quantity = uiLine.Quantity,
                    Total = uiLine.Total
                });
            }
        }
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    
    }

    private bool HasLineChanges()
    {
        // أصناف جديدة مضافة
        if (Lines.Any(l => l.Id == 0)) return true;

        // أصناف أصلية حُذفت من الـ UI
        // (يصعب تتبعها هنا، لكن SyncLinesAsync تتكفل بها)
        // نقارن المجموع كمؤشر سريع
        if (SubTotal != Lines.Where(l => l.IsOriginal).Sum(l => l.Total))
            return true;

        return false;
    }

    private string BuildChangeSummary()
    {
        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();

        if (Date != _originalDate)
            sb.AppendLine($"• التاريخ: {_originalDate:d/M/yyyy} ← {Date:d/M/yyyy}");

        if (DiscountAmount != _originalDiscountAmount)
            sb.AppendLine($"• الخصم: {_originalDiscountAmount:N2} ← {DiscountAmount:N2} جنيه");

        if (TotalAmount != _originalTotalAmount)
            sb.AppendLine($"• الإجمالي: {_originalTotalAmount:N2} ← {TotalAmount:N2} جنيه");

        if (Notes.Trim() != _originalNotes.Trim())
            sb.AppendLine("• الملاحظات: تم التعديل");

        if (Lines.Any(l => l.Id == 0))
            sb.AppendLine($"• أصناف جديدة مضافة: {Lines.Count(l => l.Id == 0)}");

        return sb.Length > 0 ? sb.ToString().TrimEnd() : "تعديلات في تفاصيل الأصناف";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void Cancel()
    {
        // تُغلق النافذة من code-behind عند الضغط على إلغاء
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  صنف معدَّل في الفاتورة
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class EditableInvoiceLine : ObservableObject
{
    public int Id { get; set; }
    public int? BloodBagId { get; set; }
    public bool IsManual { get; set; }
    /// <summary>true = صنف أصلي من DB | false = مضاف جديد</summary>
    public bool IsOriginal { get; set; }

    [ObservableProperty] private string _productName = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(Total))]
    private decimal _unitPrice;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(Total))]
    private int _quantity = 1;

    public decimal Total => UnitPrice * Quantity;

    /// <summary>الأصناف المرتبطة بكيس دم لا تُعدَّل ولا تُحذف</summary>
    public bool IsEditable => !BloodBagId.HasValue;
}

<file path="ViewModels/TrialViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class TrialViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private string _activationKey = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _machineId;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasError;

    // FIX: اسم مختلف عن StatusMessage في ViewModelBase لتفادي التعارض
    [ObservableProperty] private string _trialStatus = string.Empty;

    // FIX: نص زر النسخ — بيتغير لحظة الضغط ويرجع تاني بعد ثانيتين
    [ObservableProperty] private string _copyButtonText = "📋 نسخ";

    public int DaysRemaining { get; } = TrialManager.DaysRemaining();
    public bool IsExpired { get; } = TrialManager.DaysRemaining() == 0;

    public event Action? ActivationSucceeded;

    private readonly DispatcherTimer _copyResetTimer;

    public TrialViewModel()
    {
        _machineId = TrialManager.GetMachineId();

        _copyResetTimer = new DispatcherTimer
        {
            Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2)
        };
        _copyResetTimer.Tick += (_, _) =>
        {
            CopyButtonText = "📋 نسخ";
            TrialStatus = string.Empty;
            _copyResetTimer.Stop();
        };
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void Activate()
    {
        HasError = false;
        TrialStatus = string.Empty;

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ActivationKey))
        {
            HasError = true;
            TrialStatus = "أدخل كود التفعيل";
            return;
        }

        if (TrialManager.Activate(ActivationKey))
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                "✅ تم تفعيل البرنامج بنجاح!\nشكراً لثقتكم في شريان.",
                "تم التفعيل",
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Information);

            ActivationSucceeded?.Invoke();
        }
        else
        {
            HasError = true;
            TrialStatus = "كود التفعيل غير صحيح. تأكد من الكود وحاول مجدداً.";
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CopyMachineId()
    {
        Clipboard.SetText(MachineId);

        // FIX: تغيير فوري لزر النسخ + رسالة نجاح خضراء
        CopyButtonText = "✅ تم";
        TrialStatus = "✓ تم نسخ معرّف الجهاز";
        HasError = false;

        _copyResetTimer.Stop();
        _copyResetTimer.Start();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ContinueTrial()
    {
        ActivationSucceeded?.Invoke();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/EditInvoiceWindow.xaml.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

// لازم يكون
public partial class EditInvoiceWindow : Window
{
    private readonly EditInvoiceViewModel _vm;

    public EditInvoiceWindow(int invoiceId)
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        _vm = new EditInvoiceViewModel(invoiceId);
        DataContext = _vm;

        // زر الإلغاء — إغلاق بدون حفظ
        BtnCancel.Click += (_, _) =>
        {
            DialogResult = false;
            Close();
        };

        // بعد الحفظ الناجح → أغلق النافذة تلقائياً
        // نراقب IsBusy: لما يرجع false و Saved = true → أغلق
        _vm.PropertyChanged += (_, e) =>
        {
            if (e.PropertyName == nameof(EditInvoiceViewModel.IsBusy)
                && !_vm.IsBusy
                && _vm.Saved)
            {
                var timer = new System.Windows.Threading.DispatcherTimer
                {
                    Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(0.5)
                };
                timer.Tick += (_, _) =>
                {
                    timer.Stop();
                    DialogResult = true;
                    Close();
                };
                timer.Start();
            }
        };

        // إغلاق بضغط Escape
        KeyDown += (_, e) =>
        {
            if (e.Key == System.Windows.Input.Key.Escape && !_vm.IsBusy)
            {
                DialogResult = false;
                Close();
            }
        };
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// فتح نافذة التعديل كـ Dialog.
    /// يُعيد true إذا تم الحفظ بنجاح، false إذا أُلغي.
    /// </summary>
    public static bool ShowEdit(int invoiceId, Window? owner = null)
    {
        var win = new EditInvoiceWindow(invoiceId);
        if (owner is not null) win.Owner = owner;
        return win.ShowDialog() == true && ((EditInvoiceViewModel)win.DataContext).Saved;
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/InputDialog.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

/// <summary>
/// نافذة إدخال نص بسيطة — تُستخدم لطلب سبب الإلغاء وما شابهه.
/// تُعيد null إذا ضغط المستخدم إلغاء، أو النص المُدخل إذا أكّد.
/// </summary>
public static class InputDialog
{
    public static string? Show(string prompt, string title = "إدخال", Window? owner = null)
    {
        var dlg = new Window
        {
            Title = title,
            Width = 420,
            Height = 200,
            WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
            Owner = owner,
            ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize,
            FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft,
            WindowStyle = WindowStyle.ToolWindow
        };

        var grid = new Grid { Margin = new Thickness(20) };
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(8) });
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(12) });
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });

        var lbl = new TextBlock
        {
            Text = prompt,
            TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
            FontSize = 14
        };
        Grid.SetRow(lbl, 0);

        var txt = new TextBox
        {
            FontSize = 14,
            Padding = new Thickness(6),
            MaxLength = 200
        };
        Grid.SetRow(txt, 2);

        var btnPanel = new StackPanel
        {
            Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
            HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
            
        };
        Grid.SetRow(btnPanel, 4);

        string? result = null;

        var btnOk = new Button
        {
            Content = "تأكيد",
            Width = 90,
            Height = 34,
            FontSize = 13,
            IsDefault = true
        };
        btnOk.Click += (_, _) =>
        {
            result = txt.Text;
            dlg.DialogResult = true;
        };

        var btnCancel = new Button
        {
            Content = "إلغاء",
            Width = 90,
            Height = 34,
            FontSize = 13,
            IsCancel = true
        };
        btnCancel.Click += (_, _) => dlg.DialogResult = false;
        btnCancel.Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 0, 0);

        btnPanel.Children.Add(btnOk);
        btnPanel.Children.Add(btnCancel);

        grid.Children.Add(lbl);
        grid.Children.Add(txt);
        grid.Children.Add(btnPanel);

        dlg.Content = grid;
        dlg.Loaded += (_, _) => txt.Focus();

        var confirmed = dlg.ShowDialog();
        return confirmed == true ? result : null;
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/InvoiceView.xaml.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class InvoiceView : UserControl
{
    public InvoiceView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        // ??? ????? ??? ????? ??? ????? ???? ???????? ????????
        Loaded += (_, _) =>
        {
            TxtScanInvoice.Focus();
            ActivateInvoiceScanBorder();
        };

        TxtScanInvoice.GotFocus += (_, _) => ActivateInvoiceScanBorder();
        TxtScanInvoice.LostFocus += (_, _) => DeactivateInvoiceScanBorder();
    }

    // ??? ??? ?????? ???????? ????????????????????????????????
    private void InvoiceScanIcon_Click(object sender,
        System.Windows.Input.MouseButtonEventArgs e)
    {
        TxtScanInvoice.Focus();
        TxtScanInvoice.SelectAll();
    }

    private void ActivateInvoiceScanBorder()
    {
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B));
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(2);
        InvoiceScanIcon.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B));
    }

    private void DeactivateInvoiceScanBorder()
    {
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xE5, 0xE8, 0xEC));
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.5);
        InvoiceScanIcon.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x1A, 0x27, 0x44));
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/OrganizationSettingsView.xaml.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class OrganizationSettingsView : UserControl
{
    public OrganizationSettingsView() => InitializeComponent();

    // PasswordBox لا يدعم Binding مباشر لأسباب أمنية — نمرر القيمة عبر Code-behind
    private void OnChangePinClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (DataContext is not OrgSettingsViewModel vm) return;
        vm.ChangePinFromView(CurrentPinBox.Password, NewPinBox.Password);
        CurrentPinBox.Clear();
        NewPinBox.Clear();
    }

    private void OnRemovePinClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (DataContext is not OrgSettingsViewModel vm) return;
        vm.RemovePinFromView(CurrentPinBox.Password);
        CurrentPinBox.Clear();
    }
}

<file path="Views/PinDialog.xaml.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class PinDialog : Window
{
    public string EnteredPin { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public PinDialog()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        OkBtn.Click += (_, _) => Confirm();
        CancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => { DialogResult = false; Close(); };
        PinBox.KeyDown += (_, e) => { if (e.Key == Key.Return) Confirm(); };
        Loaded += (_, _) => PinBox.Focus();

        // السحب من الشريط الفارغ
        MouseLeftButtonDown += (_, e) => { if (e.ButtonState == MouseButtonState.Pressed) DragMove(); };
    }

    private void Confirm()
    {
        ErrorText.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;   // Hidden لا Collapsed للحفاظ على المساحة
        EnteredPin = PinBox.Password;
        DialogResult = true;
        Close();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// عرض الحوار وإرجاع الرمز المُدخَل، أو null عند الإلغاء.
    /// </summary>
    public static string? Show(Window? owner = null)
    {
        var dlg = new PinDialog();
        if (owner is not null) dlg.Owner = owner;
        return dlg.ShowDialog() == true ? dlg.EnteredPin : null;
    }

    /// <summary>يُظهر رسالة الخطأ ويمسح الحقل (للاستخدام الخارجي)</summary>
    public void ShowError()
    {
        ErrorText.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
        PinBox.Clear();
        PinBox.Focus();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Converters/EnumDisplayConverter.cs">
// Converters/EnumDisplayConverter.cs
using System;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

public class EnumDisplayConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
    {
        return value switch
        {
            // CustomerType
            CustomerType.Hospital => "مستشفى",
            CustomerType.Person => "فرد",

            // PaymentType
            PaymentType.Cash => "نقدي",
            PaymentType.Credit => "آجل",
            PaymentType.Partial => "جزئي",
            PaymentType.Electronic => "إلكتروني",
            ElectronicPaymentMethod.BankTransfer => "تحويل بنكي",
            ElectronicPaymentMethod.InstaPay => "إنستا باي",
            ElectronicPaymentMethod.EWallet => "محفظة إلكترونية",

            // SafeType
            SafeType.Cash => "خزينة نقدية",
            SafeType.Bank => "بنك",

            // BloodBagStatus
            BloodBagStatus.Available => "متاح",
            BloodBagStatus.Sold => "مباع",
            BloodBagStatus.Expired => "منتهي الصلاحية",
            BloodBagStatus.Damaged => "تالف",
            BloodBagStatus.Reserved => "محجوز",

            // VoucherType
            VoucherType.Receipt => "سند قبض",
            VoucherType.Payment => "سند صرف",

            // BloodGroup
            BloodGroup.APositive => "A+",
            BloodGroup.ANegative => "A-",
            BloodGroup.BPositive => "B+",
            BloodGroup.BNegative => "B-",
            BloodGroup.ABPositive => "AB+",
            BloodGroup.ABNegative => "AB-",
            BloodGroup.OPositive => "O+",
            BloodGroup.ONegative => "O-",

            _ => value?.ToString() ?? string.Empty
        };
    }

    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => throw new NotImplementedException();
}

</file>

<file path="Data/AppDbContext.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using System.Reflection.Emit;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Data;

public class AppDbContext : DbContext
{
    public DbSet<Customer> Customers { get; set; }
    public DbSet<Safe> Safes { get; set; }
    public DbSet<BloodBag> BloodBags { get; set; }
    public DbSet<Invoice> Invoices { get; set; }
    public DbSet<InvoiceLine> InvoiceLines { get; set; }
    public DbSet<ReceiptVoucher> ReceiptVouchers { get; set; }
    public DbSet<JournalEntry> JournalEntries { get; set; }
    public DbSet<OrganizationSettings> OrgSettings { get; set; }   // ← جديد
    public DbSet<InvoiceAuditLog> InvoiceAuditLogs => Set<InvoiceAuditLog>();

    public DbSet<ElectronicPaymentDetail> ElectronicPaymentDetails { get; set; }
    public DbSet<BloodProduct> BloodProducts { get; set; }
    public DbSet<BloodStock> BloodStocks { get; set; }
    public DbSet<ProductCatalogItem> ProductCatalogItems { get; set; }
    protected override void OnConfiguring(DbContextOptionsBuilder optionsBuilder)
    {
        var config = new ConfigurationBuilder()
            .SetBasePath(AppContext.BaseDirectory)
            .AddJsonFile("appsettings.json", optional: false, reloadOnChange: false)
            .Build();

        optionsBuilder.UseSqlServer(
            config.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection"),
            sql => sql.CommandTimeout(60));
    }

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder mb)
    {
        // ─── Customer ────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<Customer>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.Name).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.Phone).HasMaxLength(50);
            e.Property(x => x.Address).HasMaxLength(500);
            e.Property(x => x.Balance).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.CreditLimit).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.Type).HasConversion<string>();
            e.HasIndex(x => x.Name);
        });

        // ─── Safe ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<Safe>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.Name).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
            e.Property(x => x.Balance).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.Type).HasConversion<string>();
        });

        // ─── BloodBag ─────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<BloodBag>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.BagNumber).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(50);
            e.HasIndex(x => x.BagNumber).IsUnique();
            e.Property(x => x.BloodGroup).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.Status).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.CostPrice).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.SalePrice).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
        });

        // ─── Invoice ──────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<Invoice>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.InvoiceNumber).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.HasIndex(x => x.InvoiceNumber).IsUnique();
            e.Property(x => x.PaymentType).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.SubTotal).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.DiscountAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.TotalAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.PaidAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            e.HasOne(x => x.Customer)
             .WithMany(x => x.Invoices)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.CustomerId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            e.HasOne(x => x.Safe)
             .WithMany(x => x.Invoices)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.SafeId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // ─── InvoiceLine ──────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<InvoiceLine>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.ProductName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(300);
            e.Property(x => x.UnitPrice).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.Total).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            e.HasOne(x => x.Invoice)
             .WithMany(x => x.Lines)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.InvoiceId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            e.HasOne(x => x.BloodBag)
             .WithMany(x => x.InvoiceLines)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.BloodBagId)
             .IsRequired(false)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        mb.Entity<InvoiceAuditLog>(e =>
        {
            e.HasOne(a => a.Invoice)
             .WithMany()
             .HasForeignKey(a => a.InvoiceId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            e.Property(x => x.OldDiscountAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.OldTotalAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.OldPaidAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.NewDiscountAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.NewTotalAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.NewPaidAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
        });

        mb.Entity<ElectronicPaymentDetail>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.FromAccountName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.ToAccountName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.TransactionReference).HasMaxLength(100);
            e.Property(x => x.Method).HasConversion<string>();

            e.HasOne(x => x.Invoice)
             .WithOne(x => x.ElectronicDetail)
             .HasForeignKey<ElectronicPaymentDetail>(x => x.InvoiceId)
             .IsRequired(false).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            e.HasOne(x => x.ReceiptVoucher)
             .WithOne()
             .HasForeignKey<ElectronicPaymentDetail>(x => x.ReceiptVoucherId)
             .IsRequired(false).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });


        // ─── ReceiptVoucher ───────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<ReceiptVoucher>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.VoucherNumber).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.HasIndex(x => x.VoucherNumber).IsUnique();
            e.Property(x => x.Type).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.Amount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.PayeeName).HasMaxLength(200);

            e.HasOne(x => x.Safe)
             .WithMany(x => x.ReceiptVouchers)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.SafeId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            e.HasOne(x => x.Customer)
             .WithMany(x => x.ReceiptVouchers)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.CustomerId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            e.HasOne(x => x.RelatedInvoice)
             .WithMany(x => x.ReceiptVouchers)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.RelatedInvoiceId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // ─── JournalEntry ─────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<JournalEntry>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.AccountCode).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.Property(x => x.AccountName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
            e.Property(x => x.RefType).HasMaxLength(50);
            e.Property(x => x.DebitAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.CreditAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.HasIndex(x => new { x.RefType, x.RefId });

            e.HasOne(x => x.Invoice)
             .WithMany(x => x.JournalEntries)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.InvoiceId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            e.HasOne(x => x.ReceiptVoucher)
             .WithMany(x => x.JournalEntries)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.ReceiptVoucherId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // ─── OrganizationSettings ─────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<OrganizationSettings>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.BankName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.SubTitle).HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.Address).HasMaxLength(500);
            e.Property(x => x.Phone).HasMaxLength(100);
            e.Property(x => x.LogoPath).HasMaxLength(500);
            e.Property(x => x.OwnerPinHash).HasMaxLength(64);
        });



        // ─── BloodProduct ──────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<BloodProduct>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.Name).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.IsbtCode).HasMaxLength(20);
        });

        // ─── BloodStock ────────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<BloodStock>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);

            // nullable enum → string في قاعدة البيانات
            e.Property(x => x.BloodGroup).HasConversion<string>();

            e.HasOne(x => x.Product)
             .WithMany(x => x.StockEntries)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.ProductId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            // فهرس فريد للصنف + الفصيلة (مع استثناء NULL من القيد)
            // NULL يُعالَج بضمان التفرد على مستوى الكود في SaveAllAsync
            e.HasIndex(x => new { x.ProductId, x.BloodGroup })
             .IsUnique()
             .HasFilter("[BloodGroup] IS NOT NULL");
        });

        //        // ─── ProductCatalogItem ────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<ProductCatalogItem>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.Name).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.BloodGroup).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.DefaultSalePrice).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            // الفهرس الفريد: (Name, BloodGroup) — يُطبَّق على الصفوف غير الـ null فقط
            // الحالة null (N/A) تُتحكم فيها بواسطة الكود (SaveAsync يتحقق يدوياً)
            e.HasIndex(x => new { x.Name, x.BloodGroup })
             .IsUnique()
             .HasFilter("[BloodGroup] IS NOT NULL");
        });

        // ─── Seed ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<Safe>().HasData(
             new Safe { Id = 1, Name = "الخزينة الرئيسية", Type = SafeType.Cash, Balance = 0, IsMain = true },
             new Safe { Id = 2, Name = "الخزينة الإلكترونية", Type = SafeType.Electronic, Balance = 0, IsMain = false }
             );

        mb.Entity<OrganizationSettings>().HasData(
            new OrganizationSettings
            {
                Id = 1,
                BankName = "بنك الدم",
                SubTitle = "إدارة المحاسبة والمستودعات",
                Address = string.Empty,
                Phone = string.Empty
            }
        );

        mb.Entity<BloodProduct>().HasData(
            new BloodProduct { Id = 1, Name = "دم كامل", SortOrder = 1 },
            new BloodProduct { Id = 2, Name = "كرات دم مكدسة", IsbtCode = "E7428", SortOrder = 2 },
            new BloodProduct { Id = 3, Name = "بلازما طازجة مجمدة", SortOrder = 3 },
            new BloodProduct { Id = 4, Name = "كرايو - راسب أبيض", SortOrder = 4 },
            new BloodProduct { Id = 5, Name = "وحدة صفائح", IsbtCode = "E0582", SortOrder = 5 },
            new BloodProduct { Id = 6, Name = "كيس دم أطفال", SortOrder = 6 },
            new BloodProduct { Id = 7, Name = "فلترة كيس دم", SortOrder = 7 },
            new BloodProduct { Id = 8, Name = "غسيل كيس دم", SortOrder = 8 }
        );
    }
}

<file path="Helpers/JournalService.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;

public static class AccountCodes
{
    public const string Cash = "1001";   // الخزينة النقدية
    public const string Bank = "1002";   // البنك
    public const string Electronic = "1003";   // الخزينة الإلكترونية ← جديد
    public const string AccountsRec = "1100";   // ذمم مدينة (آجل)
    public const string BloodInventory = "1200";   // مخزون الدم
    public const string Sales = "4001";   // إيرادات المبيعات
    public const string COGS = "5001";   // تكلفة المبيعات
    public const string Expenses = "6001";   // المصاريف العمومية
}

public static class JournalService
{
    // ─── Helper: يستنتج كود الحساب من نوع الخزينة ─────────────
    public static string GetSafeAccountCode(Safe safe)
    {
        return safe.Type switch
        {
            SafeType.Electronic => AccountCodes.Electronic,
            SafeType.Bank => AccountCodes.Bank,
            _ => AccountCodes.Cash
        };
    }

    // ─── فاتورة نقدي أو إلكتروني ─────────────────────────────
    public static void PostInvoice(AppDbContext db, Invoice invoice, Safe safe)
    {
        var safeCode = GetSafeAccountCode(safe);
        var entries = new List<JournalEntry>();
        var date = invoice.Date;
        var refId = invoice.Id;

        if (invoice.PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash || invoice.PaymentType == PaymentType.Electronic)
        {
            entries.Add(Debit(date, safeCode, safe.Name, invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));
            entries.Add(Credit(date, AccountCodes.Sales, "إيرادات المبيعات", invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));
        }
        else // آجل أو جزئي – سيتم التعامل معه بشكل منفصل
        {
            entries.Add(Debit(date, AccountCodes.AccountsRec, $"ذمم - {invoice.Customer?.Name}", invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));
            entries.Add(Credit(date, AccountCodes.Sales, "إيرادات المبيعات", invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));
        }

        db.JournalEntries.AddRange(entries);
    }

    // ─── فاتورة جزئي ─────────────────────────────────────────
    public static void PostPartialInvoice(AppDbContext db, Invoice invoice, decimal initialPayment, Safe safe)
    {
        var safeCode = GetSafeAccountCode(safe);
        var remaining = invoice.TotalAmount - initialPayment;
        var date = invoice.Date;
        var refId = invoice.Id;
        var name = invoice.Customer?.Name ?? "";
        var entries = new List<JournalEntry>();

        if (initialPayment > 0)
            entries.Add(Debit(date, safeCode, safe.Name, initialPayment, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));

        if (remaining > 0)
            entries.Add(Debit(date, AccountCodes.AccountsRec, $"ذمم - {name}", remaining, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));

        entries.Add(Credit(date, AccountCodes.Sales, "إيرادات المبيعات", invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));

        db.JournalEntries.AddRange(entries);
    }

    // ─── سند قبض ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    public static void PostReceipt(AppDbContext db, ReceiptVoucher voucher, Safe safe)
    {
        var safeCode = GetSafeAccountCode(safe);
        var entries = new[]
        {
            Debit (voucher.Date, safeCode,          safe.Name,                          voucher.Amount, "Receipt", voucher.Id, receiptId: voucher.Id),
            Credit(voucher.Date, AccountCodes.AccountsRec, $"ذمم - {voucher.Customer?.Name}", voucher.Amount, "Receipt", voucher.Id, receiptId: voucher.Id)
        };
        db.JournalEntries.AddRange(entries);
    }

    // ─── سند دفع ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    public static void PostPayment(AppDbContext db, ReceiptVoucher voucher, Safe safe)
    {
        var safeCode = GetSafeAccountCode(safe);
        var payee = voucher.PayeeName ?? "—";
        var entries = new[]
        {
            Debit (voucher.Date, AccountCodes.Expenses, $"مصاريف - {payee}", voucher.Amount, "Payment", voucher.Id, receiptId: voucher.Id),
            Credit(voucher.Date, safeCode,              safe.Name,             voucher.Amount, "Payment", voucher.Id, receiptId: voucher.Id)
        };
        db.JournalEntries.AddRange(entries);
    }

    // ─── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    private static JournalEntry Debit(DateTime date, string code, string name, decimal amount,
        string refType, int refId, int? invoiceId = null, int? receiptId = null)
        => new()
        {
            Date = date,
            AccountCode = code,
            AccountName = name,
            DebitAmount = amount,
            CreditAmount = 0,
            RefType = refType,
            RefId = refId,
            InvoiceId = invoiceId,
            ReceiptVoucherId = receiptId,
            CreatedAt = DateTime.Now
        };

    private static JournalEntry Credit(DateTime date, string code, string name, decimal amount,
        string refType, int refId, int? invoiceId = null, int? receiptId = null)
        => new()
        {
            Date = date,
            AccountCode = code,
            AccountName = name,
            DebitAmount = 0,
            CreditAmount = amount,
            RefType = refType,
            RefId = refId,
            InvoiceId = invoiceId,
            ReceiptVoucherId = receiptId,
            CreatedAt = DateTime.Now
        };
}
</file>

<file path="Models/Invoice.cs">
namespace BloodBankAccounting.Models;


public class Invoice
{
    public int Id { get; set; }
    public string InvoiceNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public DateTime Date { get; set; } = DateTime.Now;
    public PaymentType PaymentType { get; set; }

    public int CustomerId { get; set; }
    public Customer Customer { get; set; } = null!;

    public int? SafeId { get; set; }
    public Safe? Safe { get; set; }

    public decimal SubTotal { get; set; }
    public decimal DiscountAmount { get; set; }
    public decimal TotalAmount { get; set; }
    public decimal PaidAmount { get; set; }

    // ← جديد: المتبقي = الإجمالي - المدفوع
    public decimal RemainingAmount => TotalAmount - PaidAmount;

    public bool IsCancelled { get; set; }
    public string? CancelReason { get; set; }
    public string? Notes { get; set; }
    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; } = DateTime.Now;

    public ICollection<InvoiceLine> Lines { get; set; } = [];
    public ICollection<ReceiptVoucher> ReceiptVouchers { get; set; } = [];
    public ICollection<JournalEntry> JournalEntries { get; set; } = [];
    public ElectronicPaymentDetail? ElectronicDetail { get; set; }
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/InvoicePrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class InvoicePrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColGreen = "#1E8449";
    private const string ColOrange = "#D35400";   // لون الخصم
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";   // لون الـ branding

    public static async Task PrintAsync(int invoiceId)
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var invoice = await db.Invoices
            .Include(i => i.Customer)
            .Include(i => i.Lines)
            .FirstAsync(i => i.Id == invoiceId);

        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"Invoice_{invoice.InvoiceNumber}_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMddHHmmss}.pdf");

        var doc = Document.Create(container =>
        {
            container.Page(page =>
            {
                page.Size(PageSizes.A4);
                page.Margin(2.2f, Unit.Centimetre);
                page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                    .FontFamily("Arial")
                    .FontSize(11)
                    .FontColor(ColDark)
                    .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                page.Content().Column(col =>
                {
                    // ─── Header ───────────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().Row(row =>
                    {
                        // LEFT visual: info box
                        row.ConstantItem(200)
                            .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                            .Column(inner =>
                            {
                                inner.Item()
                                    .Text("فاتورة مبيعات")
                                    .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary).AlignCenter();

                                inner.Item().PaddingTop(8).Row(r =>
                                {
                                    r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                        .Text(invoice.InvoiceNumber).Bold();
                                    r.ConstantItem(8);
                                    r.ConstantItem(80).AlignLeft()
                                        .Text("رقم الفاتورة").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                                });
                                inner.Item().PaddingTop(4).Row(r =>
                                {
                                    r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                        .Text(invoice.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                                    r.ConstantItem(8);
                                    r.ConstantItem(80).AlignLeft()
                                        .Text("التاريخ").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                                });
                            });

                        row.ConstantItem(16);

                        // RIGHT visual: bank name + optional logo
                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Row(r =>
                            {
                                if (logoBytes is not null)
                                {
                                    r.ConstantItem(52).Height(52)
                                        .Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                                    r.ConstantItem(10);
                                }
                                r.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
                                {
                                    inner.Item()
                                        .Text(org.BankName)
                                        .FontSize(logoBytes is null ? 26 : 20)
                                        .Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                                        inner.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                            .Text(org.SubTitle)
                                            .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.Phone))
                                        inner.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                            .Text($"📞  {org.Phone}")
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.Address))
                                        inner.Item()
                                            .Text(org.Address)
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                });
                            });
                        });
                    });

                    col.Item().PaddingVertical(14)
                        .LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);

                    // ─── Customer Info ────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Padding(14).Row(row =>
                    {
                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Text("رقم الهاتف")
                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignLeft();
                            c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                .Text(invoice.Customer.Phone ?? "—")
                                .FontSize(13).Bold().AlignLeft();
                        });

                        row.ConstantItem(16);
                        row.ConstantItem(1).Background(ColBorder);
                        row.ConstantItem(16);

                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Text("بيانات العميل")
                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                            c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                .Text(invoice.Customer.Name)
                                .FontSize(13).Bold().AlignRight();
                        });
                    });

                    // ─── Payment & Notes ─────────────────────────────────
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(invoice.Notes) || invoice.PaymentType != PaymentType.Cash)
                    {
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(10).Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12).Row(row =>
                            {
                                row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                                {
                                    c.Item().Text("ملاحظات")
                                        .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignLeft();
                                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                        .Text(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(invoice.Notes) ? "—" : invoice.Notes)
                                        .FontSize(11).AlignLeft();
                                });

                                row.ConstantItem(1).Background(ColBorder);
                                row.ConstantItem(16);

                                row.ConstantItem(160).Column(c =>
                                {
                                    c.Item().Text("طريقة الدفع")
                                        .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                        .Text(invoice.PaymentType switch
                                        {
                                            PaymentType.Cash => "نقدي",
                                            PaymentType.Credit => "آجل",
                                            PaymentType.Partial => $"جزئي — مدفوع: {invoice.PaidAmount:N2} ج.م",
                                            _ => invoice.PaymentType.ToString()
                                        })
                                        .FontSize(11).Bold().AlignRight();
                                });
                            });
                    }

                    // ─── Lines Table ──────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(18).Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
                    {
                        table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                        {
                            cols.ConstantColumn(100);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(2);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(2);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(4);
                            cols.ConstantColumn(36);
                        });

                        static IContainer TH(IContainer c) =>
                            c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                             .Background(ColPrimary)
                             .Padding(10).AlignCenter();

                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الإجمالي")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("سعر الوحدة")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الكمية")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الصنف / المنتج")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("#")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();

                        int i = 1;
                        foreach (var line in invoice.Lines)
                        {
                            var bg = i % 2 == 0 ? ColSurface : ColWhite;

                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text($"{line.Total:N2} ج.م").Bold().FontColor(ColGreen);
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text($"{line.UnitPrice:N2} ج.م");
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text(line.Quantity.ToString());
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignRight()
                                .Text(line.ProductName);
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text(i.ToString()).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                            i++;
                        }

                        static IContainer TD(IContainer c, string bg) =>
                            c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Background(bg).Padding(10);
                    });

                    // ─── Totals Box ───────────────────────────────────────
                    // يعرض: إجمالي الأصناف / الخصم (إن وجد) / صافي الفاتورة
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(2).Row(row =>
                    {
                        row.RelativeItem(); // spacer يدفع الجدول لليمين

                        row.ConstantItem(300)
                            .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface)
                            .Column(totals =>
                            {
                                // ── صف: إجمالي الأصناف ───────────────────
                                totals.Item()
                                    .BorderBottom(invoice.DiscountAmount > 0 ? 0.5f : 0f)
                                    .BorderColor(ColBorder)
                                    .Padding(12)
                                    .Row(r =>
                                    {
                                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                            .Text($"{invoice.SubTotal:N2} ج.م")
                                            .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColDark);
                                        r.ConstantItem(8);
                                        r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                            .Text("إجمالي الأصناف")
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                                    });

                                // ── صف: الخصم — يظهر فقط إذا كان > 0 ────
                                if (invoice.DiscountAmount > 0)
                                {
                                    totals.Item()
                                        .BorderBottom(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                                        .Padding(12)
                                        .Row(r =>
                                        {
                                            r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                                .Text(t =>
                                                {
                                                    t.Span("− ").FontColor(ColOrange).Bold();
                                                    t.Span($"{invoice.DiscountAmount:N2} ج.م")
                                                        .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColOrange);
                                                });
                                            r.ConstantItem(8);
                                            r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                                .Text("الخصم")
                                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                                        });
                                }

                                // ── صف: صافي الفاتورة ────────────────────
                                totals.Item()
                                    .Background(ColPrimary)
                                    .Padding(14)
                                    .Row(r =>
                                    {
                                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                            .Text($"{invoice.TotalAmount:N2} ج.م")
                                            .FontSize(15).Bold().FontColor(ColWhite);
                                        r.ConstantItem(8);
                                        r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                            .Text("صافي الفاتورة")
                                            .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColWhite).AlignCenter();
                                    });
                            });
                    });

                    // ─── Thank-you + Branding ─────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(20).AlignCenter()
                        .Text("شكراً لتعاملكم معنا")
                        .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColSecondary);

                    col.Item().PaddingTop(8).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor("#EEEEEE");

                    col.Item().PaddingTop(6).AlignCenter()
                        .Text(t =>
                        {
                            t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand);
                            t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                            t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand);
                        });
                });
            });
        });

        doc.GeneratePdf(path);
        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
            new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
    }
}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/PaymentVoucherViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.Printing;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;


namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class PaymentVoucherViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private string _voucherNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _date = DateTime.Today;

    /// <summary>اسم المستلم (موظف / مورّد / جهة)</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private string _payeeName = string.Empty;

    /// <summary>البيان — وصف سبب الصرف</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private string? _notes;

    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _amount;
    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;

    public ObservableCollection<Safe> Safes { get; } = [];

    /// <summary>رقم السند المحفوظ — يُستخدم لطباعة السند بعد الحفظ</summary>
    private int? _savedVoucherId;

    public bool CanPrint => _savedVoucherId.HasValue;

    public PaymentVoucherViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var safes = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();
        Safes.Clear(); safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);
        VoucherNumber = await NumberGenerator.NextPaymentVoucherNumberAsync(db);
        SelectedSafe = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain);
        _savedVoucherId = null;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanPrint));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (!Validate()) return;

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            var voucher = new ReceiptVoucher
            {
                VoucherNumber = VoucherNumber,
                Type = VoucherType.Payment,
                Date = Date,
                SafeId = SelectedSafe!.Id,
                PayeeName = PayeeName.Trim(),
                Amount = Amount,
                Notes = Notes
            };

            db.ReceiptVouchers.Add(voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // خصم المبلغ من الخزينة (المال يخرج)
            var safe = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
            safe!.Balance -= Amount;

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // قيد المحاسبة: مصاريف مدين — خزينة دائن
            JournalService.PostPayment(db, voucher, SelectedSafe!);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            await tx.CommitAsync();

            _savedVoucherId = voucher.Id;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanPrint));
            ShowSuccess($"تم حفظ سند الدفع {VoucherNumber} ✓");
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintAsync()
    {
        if (!_savedVoucherId.HasValue) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try { await PaymentPrinter.PrintAsync(_savedVoucherId.Value); }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task NewVoucherAsync()
    {
        PayeeName = string.Empty;
        Notes = null;
        Amount = 0;
        await LoadAsync();
    }

    private bool Validate()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(PayeeName)) { ShowError("أدخل اسم المستلم"); return false; }
        if (SelectedSafe is null) { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
        if (Amount <= 0) { ShowError("المبلغ يجب أن يكون أكبر من صفر"); return false; }

        // تحقق من الرصيد الكافي
        if (SelectedSafe.Balance < Amount)
        {
            ShowError($"رصيد الخزينة غير كافٍ\nالرصيد الحالي: {SelectedSafe.Balance:N2} ج.م\nالمطلوب صرفه: {Amount:N2} ج.م");
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
</file>

<UserControl x:Class="BloodBankAccounting.Views.DashboardView"
             xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation"
             xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml">

    <!--
        ✅ التغييرات للتجاوب:
        1. ScrollViewer Padding: "24,20,24,32" → "18,14,18,24"
           يترك مساحة أكبر للمحتوى على الشاشات الأصغر
        2. UniformGrid Columns="4": يظل 4 أعمدة — الـ ScrollViewer يتكفل بباقي التجاوب
           كل كارد يحصل على 25% من العرض المتاح تلقائياً
        3. أقسام الرسوم البيانية والمؤشرات: تستخدم "*" columns بالفعل → ممتازة التجاوب
        4. Margin داخل UniformGrid: خُفِّف قليلاً
    -->

    <ScrollViewer VerticalScrollBarVisibility="Auto"
                  Padding="18,14,18,24">
        <StackPanel>

            <!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
                 زر القفل / فتح البيانات المالية
            ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
            <Border Margin="0,0,0,20" HorizontalAlignment="Right">
                <Button Command="{Binding ToggleFinancialLockCommand}"
                        Cursor="Hand" BorderThickness="0" Padding="0" Background="Transparent">
                    <Button.Template>
                        <ControlTemplate TargetType="Button">
                            <Border x:Name="lockBorder" CornerRadius="10" Padding="14,9"
                                    Background="#1A2744">
                                <StackPanel Orientation="Horizontal">
                                    <TextBlock Text="{Binding LockIcon}" FontSize="15"
                                               VerticalAlignment="Center" Margin="0,0,8,0"/>
                                    <TextBlock Text="{Binding LockButtonText}"
                                               Foreground="White" FontSize="13"
                                               FontWeight="SemiBold" VerticalAlignment="Center"/>
                                </StackPanel>
                            </Border>
